    %43 = moore.read %iclk_reset_i_1 : <l1>

      
    %44 = moore.read %oclk_i_2 : <l1>

  moore.module private @bsg_async_ptr_gray(in %w_clk_i : !moore.l1, in %w_reset_i : !moore.l1, in %w_inc_i : !moore.l1, in %r_clk_i : !moore.l1, out w_ptr_binary_r_o : !moore.l64, out w_ptr_gray_r_o : !moore.l64, out w_ptr_gray_r_rsync_o : !moore.l64) {

      
    %w_clk_i_0 = moore.net name "w_clk_i" wire : <l1>

      
    %w_reset_i_1 = moore.net name "w_reset_i" wire : <l1>

      
    %w_inc_i_2 = moore.net name "w_inc_i" wire : <l1>

      
    %r_clk_i_3 = moore.net name "r_clk_i" wire : <l1>

      
    %w_ptr_binary_r_o = moore.net wire : <l64>

      
    %w_ptr_gray_r_o = moore.net wire : <l64>

      
    %w_ptr_gray_r_rsync_o = moore.net wire : <l64>

      
    %w_ptr_r = moore.variable : <l64>

      
    %w_ptr_n = moore.variable : <l64>

      
    %w_ptr_p1_r = moore.variable : <l64>

      
    %w_ptr_p1_n = moore.variable : <l64>

      
    %w_ptr_p2 = moore.variable : <l64>

      
    %w_ptr_gray_n = moore.variable : <l64>

      
    %w_ptr_gray_r = moore.variable : <l64>

      
    %w_ptr_gray_r_rsync = moore.variable : <l64>

      
    %0 = moore.read %w_ptr_p1_r : <l64>

      
    %1 = moore.constant 1 : i1

      
    %2 = moore.constant 1 : i64

      
    %3 = moore.constant 1 : l64

      
    %4 = moore.add %0, %3 : l64

      
    moore.assign %w_ptr_p2, %4 : l64

      
    %5 = moore.read %w_inc_i_2 : <l1>

      
    %6 = moore.conditional %5 : l1 -> l64 {

      
      %21 = moore.read %w_ptr_p1_r : <l64>

      
      moore.yield %21 : l64

      
    } {

      
      %21 = moore.read %w_ptr_r : <l64>

      
      moore.yield %21 : l64

      
    }

      
    moore.assign %w_ptr_n, %6 : l64

      
    %7 = moore.read %w_inc_i_2 : <l1>

      
    %8 = moore.conditional %7 : l1 -> l64 {

      
      %21 = moore.read %w_ptr_p2 : <l64>

      
      moore.yield %21 : l64

      
    } {

      
      %21 = moore.read %w_ptr_p1_r : <l64>

      
      moore.yield %21 : l64

      
    }

      
    moore.assign %w_ptr_p1_n, %8 : l64

      
    %9 = moore.read %w_inc_i_2 : <l1>

      
    %10 = moore.conditional %9 : l1 -> l64 {

      
      %21 = moore.read %w_ptr_p1_r : <l64>

      
      %22 = moore.constant 1 : i32

      
      %23 = moore.shr %21, %22 : l64, i32

      
      %24 = moore.read %w_ptr_p1_r : <l64>

      
      %25 = moore.xor %23, %24 : l64

      
      moore.yield %25 : l64

      
    } {

      
      %21 = moore.read %w_ptr_gray_r : <l64>

      
      moore.yield %21 : l64

      
    }

      
    moore.assign %w_ptr_gray_n, %10 : l64

      
    moore.procedure always {

      
      moore.wait_event {

      
        %32 = moore.read %w_clk_i_0 : <l1>

      
        moore.detect_event posedge %32 : l1

      
      }

      
      %21 = moore.read %w_reset_i_1 : <l1>

      
      %22 = moore.logic_to_int %21 : l1

      
      %23 = moore.to_builtin_int %22 : i1

      
      cf.cond_br %23, ^bb1, ^bb2

      
    ^bb1:  // pred: ^bb0

      
      %24 = moore.constant 0 : i32

      
      %25 = moore.constant 0 : i64

      
      %26 = moore.constant 0 : l64

      
      moore.nonblocking_assign %w_ptr_r, %26 : l64

      
      %27 = moore.constant 1 : i32

      
      %28 = moore.constant 1 : i64

      
      %29 = moore.constant 1 : l64

      
      moore.nonblocking_assign %w_ptr_p1_r, %29 : l64

      
      cf.br ^bb3

      
    ^bb2:  // pred: ^bb0

      
      %30 = moore.read %w_ptr_n : <l64>

      
      moore.nonblocking_assign %w_ptr_r, %30 : l64

      
      %31 = moore.read %w_ptr_p1_n : <l64>

      
      moore.nonblocking_assign %w_ptr_p1_r, %31 : l64

      
      cf.br ^bb3

      
    ^bb3:  // 2 preds: ^bb1, ^bb2

      
      moore.return

      
    }

      
    %11 = moore.read %w_ptr_r : <l64>

      
    moore.assign %w_ptr_binary_r_o, %11 : l64

      
    %12 = moore.read %w_clk_i_0 : <l1>

      
    %13 = moore.read %w_reset_i_1 : <l1>

      
    %14 = moore.read %r_clk_i_3 : <l1>

      
    %15 = moore.read %w_ptr_gray_n : <l64>

      
    %ptr_sync.iclk_data_o, %ptr_sync.oclk_data_o = moore.instance "ptr_sync" @bsg_launch_sync_sync(iclk_i: %12: !moore.l1, iclk_reset_i: %13: !moore.l1, oclk_i: %14: !moore.l1, iclk_data_i: %15: !moore.l64) -> (iclk_data_o: !moore.l64, oclk_data_o: !moore.l64)

      
    moore.assign %w_ptr_gray_r, %ptr_sync.iclk_data_o : l64

      
    moore.assign %w_ptr_gray_r_rsync, %ptr_sync.oclk_data_o : l64

      
    %16 = moore.read %w_ptr_gray_r : <l64>

      
    moore.assign %w_ptr_gray_r_o, %16 : l64

      
    %17 = moore.read %w_ptr_gray_r_rsync : <l64>

      
    moore.assign %w_ptr_gray_r_rsync_o, %17 : l64

      
    moore.assign %w_clk_i_0, %w_clk_i : l1

      
    moore.assign %w_reset_i_1, %w_reset_i : l1

      
    moore.assign %w_inc_i_2, %w_inc_i : l1

      
    moore.assign %r_clk_i_3, %r_clk_i : l1

      
    %18 = moore.read %w_ptr_binary_r_o : <l64>

      
    %19 = moore.read %w_ptr_gray_r_o : <l64>

      
    %20 = moore.read %w_ptr_gray_r_rsync_o : <l64>

      
    moore.output %18, %19, %20 : !moore.l64, !moore.l64, !moore.l64

      
  }

  moore.module private @bsg_launch_sync_sync_posedge_8_unit(in %iclk_i : !moore.l1, in %iclk_reset_i : !moore.l1, in %oclk_i : !moore.l1, in %iclk_data_i : !moore.l8, out iclk_data_o : !moore.l8, out oclk_data_o : !moore.l8) {

      
    %iclk_i_0 = moore.net name "iclk_i" wire : <l1>

      
    %iclk_reset_i_1 = moore.net name "iclk_reset_i" wire : <l1>

      
    %oclk_i_2 = moore.net name "oclk_i" wire : <l1>

      
    %iclk_data_i_3 = moore.net name "iclk_data_i" wire : <l8>

      
    %iclk_data_o = moore.net wire : <l8>

      
    %oclk_data_o = moore.net wire : <l8>

      
    %bsg_SYNC_LNCH_r = moore.variable : <l8>

      
    %bsg_SYNC_1_r = moore.variable : <l8>

      
    %bsg_SYNC_2_r = moore.variable : <l8>

      
    %0 = moore.read %bsg_SYNC_LNCH_r : <l8>

      
    moore.assign %iclk_data_o, %0 : l8

      
    moore.procedure always_ff {

      
      moore.wait_event {

      
        %12 = moore.read %iclk_i_0 : <l1>

      
        moore.detect_event posedge %12 : l1

      
      }

      
      %4 = moore.read %iclk_reset_i_1 : <l1>

      
      %5 = moore.logic_to_int %4 : l1

      
      %6 = moore.to_builtin_int %5 : i1

      
      cf.cond_br %6, ^bb1, ^bb2

      
    ^bb1:  // pred: ^bb0

      
      %7 = moore.constant 0 : i1

      
      %8 = moore.concat %7 : (!moore.i1) -> i1

      
      %9 = moore.replicate %8 : i1 -> i8

      
      %10 = moore.int_to_logic %9 : i8

      
      moore.nonblocking_assign %bsg_SYNC_LNCH_r, %10 : l8

      
      cf.br ^bb3

      
    ^bb2:  // pred: ^bb0

      
      %11 = moore.read %iclk_data_i_3 : <l8>

    %10 = moore.constant 0 : l32

      
    %11 = moore.sgt %8, %10 : l32 -> l1

      
    %12 = moore.logic_to_int %11 : l1

      
    %13 = moore.to_builtin_int %12 : i1

      
    cf.cond_br %13, ^bb2, ^bb4

      
  ^bb2:  // pred: ^bb1

      
    %14 = moore.read %0 : <l32>

      
    %15 = moore.constant 1 : i32

      
    %16 = moore.shr %14, %15 : l32, i32

      
    moore.blocking_assign %0, %16 : l32

      
    cf.br ^bb3

      
  ^bb3:  // pred: ^bb2

      
    %17 = moore.read %1 : <l32>

      
    %18 = moore.constant 1 : i32

      
    %19 = moore.constant 1 : l32

      
    %20 = moore.add %17, %19 : l32

      
    moore.blocking_assign %1, %20 : l32

      
    cf.br ^bb1

      
  ^bb4:  // pred: ^bb1

      
    %21 = moore.read %1 : <l32>

      
    return %21 : !moore.l32

      
  }

      
}
